   Radio Receiver R-394/U is a 22-tube double      nance is rapid and relatively easy because
conversion superheterodyne receiver. It oper-      plug-in type assemblies are used for all the
ates in the frequency range of 152 to 174 mega-    stages. Ordinarily, a defective receiver can be

verting the audio output signals to sound          be used.
waves.

quency-modulated (fm) signals and converting       strength as well as to provide more uniform

19. Volume Adjustment                                   Figure 15.                                  TMS092=575

   The volume of the receiver is adjusted by                        Radio Receiver R-394/U, first high-frequency
advancing the VOLUME switch clockwise for                                    oscillator crystai oven.
more output. The operator has an option of
four audio volume levels, the lowest being at a
position one step clockwise from the OFF posi-
tion, and the maximum at the fully clockwise
position. Adjust the switch for a comfortable
level of sound.

20. Squelch Adjustment

   a. When the SQUELCH control is in the OFF
position, the receiver is unsquelched. This per-
mits the receiver to receive any strength signal
without cutting off the audio output. The
SQUELCH control can be adjusted to mute the
receiver output when a weak signal or no signal
is being received. The setting at which this
occurs is called the squelch threshold level.

   b. Adjust the SQUELCH control as follows:

       (1) Turn the SQUELCH control to its fully
              clockwise position (not OFF) and note
              whether a signal or just noise is being
              received.

        (2) At an interval when no signal is heard,
              turn the SQUELCH control counter-
               clockwise until the background noise
              disappears, but no further.

        (3) When the noise level is high, and signal
              strength low, it may be desirable to
              operate with the SQUELCH control in
              the OFF position. Otherwise there will
              be intermittent or no reception. If the
               squelch circuit behavior is erratic and
              repair on this circuit is not practical,
               the squelch circuit can be disabled
              effectively by setting the SQUELCH
              control to the OFF position.

21. Stopping Procedure

   a. Rotate the SQUELCH control fully clock-
wise until a click is heard.

   b. Rotate the VOLUME control fully counter-
clockwise to the OFF position.

    Note. The resonant frequency of some crystals may be higher than their nominal rated frequencies. When one of these crystals is warped
(by adjustment of Z31) to the desired frequency, the frequency of oscillation may be considerably lower than the natural frequency of the
crystal. Under these conditions, the oscillator may not oscillate once the receiver is turned off and later turned on. Therefore, turn the
receiver off and then on at least once after the final adjustment of the LOCAL OSC 152-174 MC plug-in unit. If the oscillator fails to func-
tion subsequent to this test, replace crystal Y31 with one that will give satisfactory operation. (Crystal Y31 is a type CR-32/U.)
